Table legend: (Time is in decaauts)\n A = Elapsed time spent in this place.\n B = Non-inter-level travel time spent in this place.\n C = Inter-level travel time spent in this place.\n D = Time resting spent in this place.\n E = Time spent auto-exploring this place.\n F = Levels seen in this place.\n G = Mean time per level.\n
表格说明：（时间单位为 decaaut）\n A = 在此地点消耗的总时间。\n B = 在此地点非跨层移动消耗的时间。\n C = 在此地点跨层移动消耗的时间。\n D = 在此地点休息消耗的时间。\n E = 在此地点自动探索消耗的时间。\n F = 在此地点探索过的层数。\n G = 每层的平均时间。\n

%8s: %d daAuts

%%%%
Table legend:\n A = Non-vault XP\n B = Vault XP\n C = Vault XP percentage of total XP\n D = Non-vault monster count\n E = Vault monster count\n F = Vault count percentage of total count\n\n
表格说明：\n A = 非宝库经验值\n B = 宝库经验值\n C = 宝库经验值占总经验值的百分比\n D = 非宝库怪物数量\n E = 宝库怪物数量\n F = 宝库数量占总数的百分比\n\n

%%%%
Table legend:\n A = Kills in this place as a percentage of kills in the entire game.\n B = Kills by you in this place as a percentage of kills by you in\n     the entire game.\n C = Kills by friends in this place as a percentage of kills by\n     friends in the entire game.\n D = Other kills in this place as a percentage of other kills in the\n     entire game.\n E = Experience gained in this place as a percentage of experience\n     gained in the entire game.\n F = Experience gained in this place divided by the number of levels of\n     this place that you have seen.\n\n
表格说明：\n A = 此地点的击杀数占整个游戏击杀数的百分比。\n B = 此地点的由你造成的击杀数占整个游戏中你造成的击杀数的\n     百分比。\n C = 此地点的由友方造成的击杀数占整个游戏中友方造成的击杀数\n     的百分比。\n D = 此地点的其他击杀数占整个游戏中其他击杀数的百分比。\n E = 此地点的经验值占整个游戏经验值的百分比。\n F = 此地点的经验值除以你所探索过的此地点层数。\n\n

It radiates damaging sonic waves as it strikes, especially in combat involving dangerous or numerous foes.
它攻击时释放破坏性音波，在对抗危险或众多敌人的战斗中尤为有效。
%%%%
It causes slowness and exhaustion when equipped.
装备时造成缓慢和疲惫。
%%%%
It extends the wielder's berserk rage when killing foes.
使用者在击杀敌人时会延长自身的狂暴之怒。
%%%%
It occasionally unleashes arcs of chaos on nearby creatures upon striking a foe.
攻击敌人时会偶尔向附近生物释放混沌弧光。
%%%%
It halves damage taken while paralysed, petrified or unconscious.
其佩戴者在麻痹、石化或昏迷时受到的伤害减半。
%%%%
It can be activated via the 'a'bility menu to radiate toxic energy, with effectiveness depending on Evocations skill.
可以通过能力菜单激活，释放毒素能量，其效果取决于激活技能。
%%%%
It enhances your alchemy magic.
它增强你的炼金魔法。
%%%%
It grants immunity to poison.
它赋予毒素免疫。
%%%%
If the wielder is skilled in Evocations, they can envenom those struck by it, dealing poison damage which bypasses armour.
若使用者精通激活技能，可以在攻击时为武器淬毒，造成无视护甲的毒素伤害。
%%%%
Whenever a successful spell causes magic to be channelled from it, it may also drain additional magic from other enemies in sight, with a chance increasing with spell level.
每当成功施法从中引导魔力时，也可能从视野内的其他敌人身上汲取额外魔力，几率随法术等级提高。
%%%%
It occasionally heals its wielder for the full amount of damage dealt when it wounds a living foe.
攻击活着的敌人时，偶尔将造成伤害的全额转化为使用者的生命值。
%%%%
It inflicts powerful necromantic curses on any living foe it strikes.
对击中的任何活着的敌人施加强大的亡灵诅咒。
%%%%
It occasionally torments all nearby living foes when it strikes in combat.
战斗时攻击会偶尔折磨附近所有活着的敌人。
%%%%
It temporarily reduces the fire resistance of those it strikes.
暂时降低被击中者的火焰抗性。
%%%%
It grants the ability to hurl damnation at the cost of health and magic, with strength depending on Evocations skill.
赋予投掷天谴的能力，消耗生命与法力，威力取决于激活技能。
%%%%
It may summon demons of flame and damnation when striking foes.
攻击敌人时可能召唤火焰与天谴之恶魔。
%%%%
It surrounds you with an aura of shadow, increasing your stealth and reducing the accuracy of all others within it.
以暗影光环环绕你，提升你的潜行能力并降低光环内所有其他生物的命中率。
%%%%
It heals its wielder for a portion of the damage dealt every time it strikes a living foe.
每次攻击活着的敌人时，将造成伤害的一部分转化为使用者的生命值。
%%%%
It may instantly slay those it strikes, especially weaker foes.
可能直接斩杀被击中的敌人，尤其对较弱的对手更为有效。
%%%%
When it strikes, its spirit leaps out and fights alongside the wielder.
攻击时，其灵体跃出并与使用者并肩作战。
%%%%
Whenever the wielder enters a berserk rage, their allies are berserked alongside them.
当使用者进入狂暴之怒时，其盟友也会一同狂暴。
%%%%
Any ammunition fired from it explodes in a burst of damnation on striking a foe, dealing damage which bypasses armour.
射出的任何弹药在击中敌人时爆发出天谴之焰，造成无视护甲的伤害。
%%%%
It inflicts additional damage proportional to its victim's maximum health.
根据受害者的最大生命值比例造成额外伤害。
%%%%
It grants its wielder a halo, revealing invisible creatures and increasing accuracy against all within it other than the wielder.
赋予使用者光环，可以揭示隐形生物，并提升对光环内除使用者外所有生物的命中率。
%%%%
It enhances your fire, ice, earth and air magic. If skilled in Evocations, you can discharge powerful blasts of elemental energy when striking with it.
增强你的火焰、寒冰、大地和空气魔法。若精通激活技能，在使用其攻击时可以释放强大的元素能量冲击。
%%%%
It never misses its target.
它绝不会失手。
%%%%
It inflicts extra damage against dragons and draconians.
对龙和龙裔造成额外伤害。
%%%%
It is more effective when stabbing unaware enemies, sometimes treating them as though they were completely helpless.
在偷袭未察觉的敌人时效果更佳，有时会将其视为完全无助。
%%%%
It occasionally inflicts wild transmutation effects on those it strikes, inflicting malmutation or extra damage, or polymorphing them into a new form.
偶尔对被击中的目标施加狂野的变形效果，造成恶性突变或额外伤害，或将其变形成新形态。
%%%%
It inflicts significantly more damage against the undead.
对亡灵造成大量额外伤害。
%%%%
It occasionally inflicts asphyxiating poison on those it strikes, dealing poison damage and causing slowness.
偶尔对被击中的目标施加窒息毒药，造成毒素伤害并导致缓慢。
%%%%
It may disarm weapon-wielding foes when striking them, dealing extra damage in the process.
攻击持武器敌人时可能缴除其武器，并在过程中造成额外伤害。
%%%%
Any ammunition fired by it passes through the targets it hits, potentially hitting all targets in its path until it reaches maximum range.
射出的任何弹药都会穿透击中的目标，可能击中路径上的所有目标直至达到最大射程。
%%%%
All noise in your surroundings is dampened.
你周围的所有噪音都被抑制。
%%%%
Going berserk makes the wearer exceptionally tough, and they are slowed for a shorter time afterward.
进入狂暴状态使穿戴者异常坚韧，且狂暴后的减速时间缩短。
%%%%

使穿戴者能够感知附近的生物。
%%%%

%%%%
It radiates cold, occasionally slowing all nearby creatures who are not resistant to cold.
它散发寒气，偶尔减速附近所有不抗寒的生物。
%%%%
It surrounds you with flame clouds.
它用火焰云环绕你。
%%%%
It grants immunity to flame clouds.
它赋予对火焰云的免疫。
%%%%
It increases the power of your magical spells and halves their MP cost (rounding up).
它增强你法术的威力并将法力消耗减半（向上取整）。
%%%%
It reduces your willpower to zero.
它将你的意志力降为零。
%%%%
It occasionally summons a dragon when using Invocations with hostile monsters in sight. Chance is 10% + twice the piety cost of the ability used.
在视野内有敌对怪物时使用祈唤，偶尔会召唤一条龙。触发几率为10% + 所使用能力的虔诚消耗的两倍。
%%%%
It shrouds the wearer in darkness, reducing the range at which they can see and be seen by others.
将穿戴者笼罩于黑暗中，缩短其视野范围以及被他人看见的距离。
%%%%
It allows the wearer to riposte with melee weapons, swiftly retaliating against missed or blocked melee attacks.
使穿戴者能够使用近战武器还击，在敌人近战攻击落空或被格挡时迅速反击。
%%%%
It may blind adjacent enemies who miss an attack against the wearer in melee combat.
近战战斗中攻击穿戴者失手的相邻敌人可能会被致盲。
%%%%
When the wearer is hurt, rats may emerge from it to assist them.
当穿戴者受伤时，老鼠可能从中涌出前来协助。
%%%%
It is deafeningly noisy when blocking attacks.
格挡攻击时响声震耳欲聋。
%%%%
It reduces the accuracy of your attacks and spells.
降低你的攻击和法术的命中率。
%%%%
It extends the wearer's berserk rage when killing foes.
穿戴者在击杀敌人时会延长自身的狂暴之怒。
%%%%
It occasionally creates storm clouds above nearby enemies.
偶尔在附近敌人的头顶制造风暴云。
%%%%
It grants its wearer immunity to clouds.
赋予穿戴者对云雾的免疫。
%%%%
It occasionally summons demons to assist the wielder when it strikes in combat.
战斗时攻击偶尔召唤恶魔前来协助使用者。
%%%%
Its wielder is mesmerised by any visible foes, and it cannot be unequipped while any foes are visible.
使用者会被任何可见的敌人催眠，且在有敌人可见时无法被卸下。
%%%%
It allows the wearer to cover ground more swiftly.
使穿戴者能够更快地移动。
%%%%
It sometimes launches melee attacks against multiple visible enemies.
有时会向多个可见敌人发起近战攻击。
%%%%
It releases a powerful concussive burst when it strikes, dealing extra damage to the target and all other creatures adjacent to them.
攻击时释放强力冲击波，对目标及相邻的所有其他生物造成额外伤害。
%%%%
It grants the wearer a chance to resist fire, cold, poison, electricity, acid, and negative energy.
赋予穿戴者抵抗火焰、寒冷、毒素、电击、酸蚀和负能量的几率。
%%%%
It enables you to equip an extra ring.
使你能够装备一枚额外的戒指。
%%%%
It occasionally unleashes powerful arcs of lightning upon striking a foe. These may arc to other adjacent creatures (including the wielder, but dealing reduced damage to them) before grounding out.
攻击敌人时偶尔释放强大的闪电弧。这些闪电弧可能跳转到附近的其他生物（包括使用者，但对其伤害降低）然后接地消散。
%%%%
It unleashes wild and unpredictable magical effects upon striking a wizard or a creature with magical abilities.
攻击巫师或有魔法能力的生物时释放狂野而不可预测的魔法效果。
%%%%
It inflicts extra damage upon those it strikes, and deals substantially increased damage to chaotic, mutated and magically transformed beings.
对被击中的目标造成额外伤害，并对混沌、突变和魔法变形生物造成大幅增加的伤害。
%%%%
It fills those it strikes with an explosive inner flame.
在被击中的目标体内注入爆裂内在火焰。
%%%%
It grants immunity to clouds of flame.
它赋予对火焰云的免疫。
%%%%
It heals its wielder for a portion of the damage dealt when they wound a living foe with it or with a spell, but its wielder's spells cost health as well as magical power.
当使用者以此武器或法术伤害活着的敌人时，将造成伤害的一部分转化为生命值，但使用者的法术同时消耗生命和法力。
%%%%
It can block and reflect piercing ranged attacks and magical attacks against the wielder's willpower.
它可以阻挡并反射穿刺类远程攻击和魔法攻击，反射对抗使用者的意志力。
%%%%
It freezes victims, dealing an additional one-quarter of any damage that pierces defenders' armour. It may also slow down cold-blooded creatures.
冰冻目标，对穿透防御者护甲的任何伤害再附加四分之一。也可能减速冷血生物。
%%%%
It becomes more effective when it hits something. This effect fades over time.
击中目标后变得更有效，此效果会随时间消退。
%%%%
It can reach especially far, and its reaching attacks ignore other creatures in the way.
可以触及极远的距离，且其远程攻击无视路径上的其他生物。
%%%%
It summons a battlesphere in combat, with strength depending on Conjurations skill.
战斗中召唤战斗法球，其强度取决于咒语魔法技能。
%%%%
It enhances your destructive magic, and weakens non-destructive magic.
增强你的毁灭魔法，削弱非毁灭魔法。
%%%%
It gathers nearby corpses to temporarily increase the armour it provides.
收集附近的尸体以暂时提升其提供的护甲值。
%%%%
It protects you from foul pestilence.
保护你不受秽恶瘟疫的侵害。
%%%%

向敌人移动时，穿戴者尽可能移动最大距离，途中会惊醒目标。
%%%%
It empowers its wearer's blows with all of their magic.
将穿戴者的魔力全部灌注于其攻击之中。
%%%%
Blows struck in melee replenish the wearer's magic.
近战攻击会补充穿戴者的法力。
%%%%
It absorbs a single lethal wound into itself, holding its wearer just short of death and releasing dream-creatures to guard them.
吸收一次致命伤害，使穿戴者濒死而不亡，并释放梦境生物来守护他们。
%%%%
It heals its wearer when they drink unknown potions, and restores their MP when they read unknown scrolls.
穿戴者饮用未知药水时回复生命，阅读未知卷轴时回复法力。
%%%%
It may instantly kill foes which have little health.
可能瞬间杀死生命值较低的敌人。
%%%%
It provides extra protection to wielders with little health.
为生命值较低的使用者提供额外防护。
%%%%
It makes its victims' attacks reflect back on them.
使受害者的攻击反弹回自身。
%%%%
It prevents involuntary movement, except for blinking, teleportation, or banishment.
防止非自愿移动，但闪烁、传送和放逐除外。
%%%%
It can be evoked to extend its reach, and strikes at all foes within reach, dealing less damage to those not targeted.
可被激活以延伸触及范围，并攻击触及范围内的所有敌人，非指定目标的伤害降低。

其穿戴者无法被缠绕、被网住、被蜘蛛网困住或被束缚法印阻止。
%%%%

其穿戴者从伤害性近战攻击中滑开，将攻击者向前拉扯。

%%%%
It becomes more powerful as more dangerous foes come into view.

%%%%
When the wearer kills a threatening monster, it has a chance to gain +1 AC, +1 Slay, and +1 Int, capping at +7. When the wearer uses a scroll or a potion with a hostile monster in sight, its bonuses reset to +0. Unequipping it also resets its bonuses to +0.
穿戴者杀死威胁性怪物时，有几率获得+1护甲、+1杀戮和+1智力，上限+7。当穿戴者在有敌对怪物可见时使用卷轴或药水，其加成重置为+0。卸下它也会将加成重置为+0。
%%%%
When it blocks an attack, it may electrocute the attacker. This works even against foes who are a short distance away.
格挡攻击时可能电击攻击者。此效果对短距离外的敌人也有效。
%%%%

开火时经常将使用者向后弹射。
